`tests/protocol/test_registry.py`:

```python
import logging

import pytest

from qumidi.errors import ParameterRangeError, UnknownParameterError, UnsupportedOnModelError
from qumidi.protocol.channels import (
    CAPS_BY_BOX_ID,
    ChannelClass,
    FxSend,
    Group,
    Input,
    Matrix,
    Mix,
)
from qumidi.protocol.params import Fixed, SendTarget
from qumidi.protocol.registry import (
    REGISTRY,
    SEND_TARGET_VX,
    VX_TO_SEND_TARGET,
    send_target_vx,
)


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "target,vx",
    [
        (Mix.MIX1, 0x00), (Mix.MIX2, 0x01), (Mix.MIX3, 0x02), (Mix.MIX4, 0x03),
        (Mix.MIX56, 0x04), (Mix.MIX78, 0x05), (Mix.MIX910, 0x06),
        (Mix.LR, 0x07),
        (Group(1), 0x08), (Group(2), 0x09), (Group(3), 0x0A), (Group(4), 0x0B),
        (Matrix(1), 0x0C), (Matrix(2), 0x0D),
        (FxSend(1), 0x10), (FxSend(2), 0x11), (FxSend(3), 0x12), (FxSend(4), 0x13),
    ],
)
def test_the_single_canonical_send_target_table(target, vx) -> None:  # type: ignore[no-untyped-def]
    # PRD 14.1: the spec contradicts itself; Pan and Pre/Post resolve it.
    assert SEND_TARGET_VX[target] == vx
    assert VX_TO_SEND_TARGET[vx] == target


def test_send_targets_are_not_a_dense_range() -> None:
    # PRD 7.2: note the gap at 0E-0F. VX must come from the table by lookup,
    # never from arithmetic on a mix index.
    assert 0x0E not in VX_TO_SEND_TARGET
    assert 0x0F not in VX_TO_SEND_TARGET


def test_send_target_validates_against_model_caps() -> None:
    qu16 = CAPS_BY_BOX_ID[1]
    assert send_target_vx(Mix.MIX3, qu16) == 0x02
    with pytest.raises(UnsupportedOnModelError):
        send_target_vx(Group(1), qu16)
    with pytest.raises(UnsupportedOnModelError):
        send_target_vx(Matrix(1), qu16)
    with pytest.raises(UnsupportedOnModelError):
        send_target_vx(FxSend(3), qu16)


def test_send_target_rejects_a_channel_that_is_not_a_send_destination() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ParameterRangeError):
        send_target_vx(Input(6), None)


def test_registry_lookup_by_name() -> None:
    spec = REGISTRY.lookup("fader")
    assert spec.nrpn_id == 0x17
    assert isinstance(spec.vx, Fixed)
    assert spec.vx.value == 0x07


def test_registry_lookup_of_an_unknown_name_names_the_alternatives() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(UnknownParameterError) as excinfo:
        REGISTRY.lookup("volume")
    assert "fader" in str(excinfo.value)


def test_send_level_and_pan_use_the_send_target_vx_mode() -> None:
    assert isinstance(REGISTRY.lookup("send_level").vx, SendTarget)
    assert isinstance(REGISTRY.lookup("pan").vx, SendTarget)


def test_pan_is_bounded_at_4a_not_7f() -> None:
    # PRD 14.9: centre is 0x25, not 0x40. Any code treating pan as a signed
    # offset from 0x40 pans every channel hard left.
    domain = REGISTRY.lookup("pan").domain
    assert (domain.lo, domain.hi) == (0x00, 0x4A)  # type: ignore[attr-defined]


def test_resolve_finds_the_normal_case_by_class_and_id() -> None:
    spec = REGISTRY.resolve(ChannelClass.INPUT, 0x17, 0x07)
    assert spec is not None and spec.name == "fader"


def test_resolve_returns_none_for_an_unknown_id() -> None:
    assert REGISTRY.resolve(ChannelClass.INPUT, 0x7E, 0x07) is None


def test_resolve_falls_back_across_classes_with_a_debug_note(caplog) -> None:  # type: ignore[no-untyped-def]
    # PRD 5.3 step 3.
    with caplog.at_level(logging.DEBUG, logger="qumidi.protocol.registry"):
        spec = REGISTRY.resolve(ChannelClass.MUTE_GROUP, 0x17, 0x07)
    assert spec is not None and spec.name == "fader"
    assert any("unexpected" in r.message.lower() for r in caplog.records)


def test_resolve_warns_when_a_fixed_vx_does_not_match(caplog) -> None:  # type: ignore[no-untyped-def]
    # PRD 5.3: this is the signature of a transcription error in the registry.
    with caplog.at_level(logging.WARNING):
        spec = REGISTRY.resolve(ChannelClass.INPUT, 0x17, 0x00)
    assert spec is not None and spec.name == "fader"
    assert any("filler" in r.message.lower() for r in caplog.records)


def test_lr_assign_and_mix_assign_are_distinct_entries() -> None:
    # PRD 14.8: encode LR assignment exclusively as 18; 55/07 is rejected.
    assert REGISTRY.lookup("lr_assign").nrpn_id == 0x18
    assert REGISTRY.lookup("mix_assign").nrpn_id == 0x55


def test_params_for_a_class_excludes_parameters_that_do_not_apply() -> None:
    names = {s.name for s in REGISTRY.params_for(ChannelClass.INPUT)}
    assert "fader" in names
    assert "send_level" in names


def test_every_spec_name_is_unique_per_channel_class() -> None:
    seen: set[tuple[str, ChannelClass]] = set()
    for spec in REGISTRY:
        for cls in spec.applies_to:
            key = (spec.name, cls)
            assert key not in seen, f"{spec.name} registered twice for {cls}"
            seen.add(key)
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `qumidi/protocol/registry.py`**

```python
"""THE parameter registry (PRD 7.2).

This is the core asset. Everything else is generic machinery over it. A
parameter is described here exactly once, and both the flat command API and
the object facade are generated from these rows.

The VX filler value is inconsistent across parameters and is transcribed
literally from the spec, never inferred. Getting one wrong produces messages
the mixer silently ignores, which is nearly impossible to debug in the field
-- tests/fixtures/golden_params.yaml exists primarily to guard this table.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import logging
from collections.abc import Iterator, Sequence

from qumidi.errors import ParameterRangeError, UnknownParameterError, UnsupportedOnModelError
from qumidi.protocol.channels import (
    ChannelClass,
    ChannelId,
    FxSend,
    Group,
    Matrix,
    Mix,
    ModelCaps,
    check_channel,
)
from qumidi.protocol.params import (
    Boolean,
    Direction,
    Fixed,
    Interpolated,
    ParamSpec,
    Raw7Bit,
    SendTarget,
    Tier,
)
from qumidi.protocol.units import FADER_TAPER

log = logging.getLogger(__name__)

# --- Send targets (PRD 7.2, resolving the contradiction in PRD 14.1) ------

#: The single canonical send-target table. Used by all four send-family
#: parameters: Pan (16), Mix Pre/Post (50), Mix Assign (55), Send Level (20).
SEND_TARGET_VX: dict[ChannelId, int] = {
    Mix.MIX1: 0x00,
    Mix.MIX2: 0x01,
    Mix.MIX3: 0x02,
    Mix.MIX4: 0x03,
    Mix.MIX56: 0x04,
    Mix.MIX78: 0x05,
    Mix.MIX910: 0x06,
    Mix.LR: 0x07,
    Group(1): 0x08,
    Group(2): 0x09,
    Group(3): 0x0A,
    Group(4): 0x0B,
    Matrix(1): 0x0C,
    Matrix(2): 0x0D,
    # Note the gap at 0x0E-0x0F: send targets are NOT a dense range.
    FxSend(1): 0x10,
    FxSend(2): 0x11,
    FxSend(3): 0x12,
    FxSend(4): 0x13,
}

VX_TO_SEND_TARGET: dict[int, ChannelId] = {vx: ch for ch, vx in SEND_TARGET_VX.items()}


def send_target_vx(target: ChannelId, caps: ModelCaps | None) -> int:
    """Look up a send destination's VX byte, validated against the model.

    Never compute this from arithmetic on a mix index -- the table has a gap
    at 0x0E-0x0F (PRD 7.2).
    """
    try:
        vx = SEND_TARGET_VX[target]
    except KeyError:
        raise ParameterRangeError(
            f"{target.label} is not a send destination; valid destinations are "
            f"{', '.join(t.label for t in SEND_TARGET_VX)}"
        ) from None
    try:
        check_channel(caps, target)
    except UnsupportedOnModelError:
        raise
    return vx


# --- The registry ---------------------------------------------------------

_INPUTS = frozenset({ChannelClass.INPUT, ChannelClass.STEREO_INPUT})
_FX_RETURNS = frozenset({ChannelClass.FX_RETURN})
_MIXES = frozenset(
    {ChannelClass.MIX_MONO, ChannelClass.MIX_STEREO, ChannelClass.LR,
     ChannelClass.GROUP, ChannelClass.MATRIX}
)
_DCA = frozenset({ChannelClass.DCA})
#: Everything with a fader: inputs, stereo inputs, FX returns, DCAs, mixes.
_FADER_CLASSES = _INPUTS | _FX_RETURNS | _DCA | _MIXES
#: Everything that can be a *source* of a send.
_SEND_SOURCES = _INPUTS | _FX_RETURNS | frozenset(
    {ChannelClass.MIX_MONO, ChannelClass.MIX_STEREO, ChannelClass.GROUP}
)


SPECS: list[ParamSpec] = [
    ParamSpec(
        name="fader",
        nrpn_id=0x17,
        vx=Fixed(0x07),
        domain=Interpolated(lo=0x00, hi=0x7F, taper=FADER_TAPER),
        applies_to=_FADER_CLASSES,
    ),
    ParamSpec(
        name="send_level",
        nrpn_id=0x20,
        vx=SendTarget(),
        domain=Interpolated(lo=0x00, hi=0x7F, taper=FADER_TAPER),
        applies_to=_SEND_SOURCES,
        notes="Same taper as the fader, including the undefined 0x01-0x0B band (PRD 8.1).",
    ),
    ParamSpec(
        name="pan",
        nrpn_id=0x16,
        vx=SendTarget(),
        # PRD 14.9: centre is 0x25 and the range stops at 0x4A. NOT symmetric
        # about 0x40 -- treating it that way pans every channel hard left.
        domain=Raw7Bit(lo=0x00, hi=0x4A),
        applies_to=_SEND_SOURCES,
        notes="Centre 0x25, full left 0x00, full right 0x4A. There is no separate "
        "channel-pan parameter: a channel's LR pan is pan with VX=0x07 (PRD 7.2).",
    ),
    ParamSpec(
        name="lr_assign",
        nrpn_id=0x18,
        vx=Fixed(0x07),
        domain=Boolean(),
        applies_to=_SEND_SOURCES,
        notes="PRD 14.8: the canonical encoding for LR assignment. 55/07 is rejected "
        "on write and normalised to this key on read.",
    ),
    ParamSpec(
        name="mix_assign",
        nrpn_id=0x55,
        vx=SendTarget(),
        domain=Boolean(),
        applies_to=_SEND_SOURCES,
        notes="PRD 14.8: valid only for VX in 00-06, 08-0D, 10-13. Encoding 55/07 is a "
        "ParameterRangeError -- use lr_assign (18) for LR.",
    ),
    ParamSpec(
        name="mix_pre",
        nrpn_id=0x50,
        vx=SendTarget(),
        domain=Boolean(off=0x00, on=0x01),
        applies_to=_SEND_SOURCES,
        notes="0x00 post, 0x01 pre.",
    ),
    ParamSpec(
        name="pafl",
        nrpn_id=0x51,
        vx=Fixed(0x07),
        domain=Boolean(),
        applies_to=_FADER_CLASSES,
    ),
]


class Registry:
    """Name and wire lookups over a ParamSpec table."""

    def __init__(self, specs: Sequence[ParamSpec]) -> None:
        self._specs = list(specs)
        self._by_name: dict[tuple[str, ChannelClass], ParamSpec] = {}
        self._by_name_any: dict[str, list[ParamSpec]] = {}
        self._by_class_id: dict[tuple[ChannelClass, int], ParamSpec] = {}
        self._by_class_id_vx: dict[tuple[ChannelClass, int, int], ParamSpec] = {}
        self._by_id: dict[int, list[ParamSpec]] = {}
        for spec in self._specs:
            self._by_name_any.setdefault(spec.name, []).append(spec)
            self._by_id.setdefault(spec.nrpn_id, []).append(spec)
            for cls in spec.applies_to:
                self._by_name[(spec.name, cls)] = spec
                self._by_class_id[(cls, spec.nrpn_id)] = spec
                if isinstance(spec.vx, Fixed):
                    self._by_class_id_vx[(cls, spec.nrpn_id, spec.vx.value)] = spec

    def __iter__(self) -> Iterator[ParamSpec]:
        return iter(self._specs)

    def __len__(self) -> int:
        return len(self._specs)

    def register_exact(self, spec: ParamSpec, vx: int) -> None:
        """Index a spec under an exact (class, id, vx) key.

        Used for the FX-delay 48 collision, where coarse-time and link share
        an id and are discriminated only by VX (PRD 14.5).
        """
        for cls in spec.applies_to:
            self._by_class_id_vx[(cls, spec.nrpn_id, vx)] = spec

    def lookup(self, name: str, cls: ChannelClass | None = None) -> ParamSpec:
        """Find a spec by name, disambiguating by channel class where needed.

        delay_time is registered twice with disjoint applies_to sets because
        its range is half-scale on inputs (PRD 7.2), so ``cls`` matters there.
        """
        if cls is not None:
            spec = self._by_name.get((name, cls))
            if spec is not None:
                return spec
        candidates = self._by_name_any.get(name)
        if not candidates:
            close = ", ".join(sorted(self._by_name_any)[:12])
            raise UnknownParameterError(
                f"no parameter named {name!r}; known parameters include: {close}"
            )
        if cls is not None:
            raise UnknownParameterError(
                f"parameter {name!r} does not apply to {cls.name}; it applies to "
                f"{', '.join(sorted(c.name for s in candidates for c in s.applies_to))}"
            )
        return candidates[0]

    def resolve(self, cls: ChannelClass, param_id: int, vx: int | None) -> ParamSpec | None:
        """Resolve an inbound raw update (PRD 5.3).

        Three steps, stopping at the first hit. Keying on id alone is
        insufficient: the same id means different things on different channel
        classes, and a future firmware could reuse one.
        """
        # 1. Exact (class, id, vx) -- needed for the FX-delay 48 collision.
        if vx is not None:
            spec = self._by_class_id_vx.get((cls, param_id, vx))
            if spec is not None:
                return spec

        # 2. (class, id) -- the normal case.
        spec = self._by_class_id.get((cls, param_id))
        if spec is not None:
            if isinstance(spec.vx, Fixed) and vx is not None and vx != spec.vx.value:
                # PRD 5.3: decode it anyway, but this is the signature of a
                # transcription error in the registry.
                log.warning(
                    "%s on %s: expected the fixed filler VX %#04x but observed %#04x. "
                    "The registry entry may be mis-transcribed.",
                    spec.name, cls.name, spec.vx.value, vx,
                )
            return spec

        # 3. (id,) across all classes.
        candidates = self._by_id.get(param_id)
        if candidates:
            log.debug(
                "parameter id %#04x resolved to %r on an unexpected channel class %s",
                param_id, candidates[0].name, cls.name,
            )
            return candidates[0]
        return None

    def params_for(self, cls: ChannelClass) -> list[ParamSpec]:
        return [spec for spec in self._specs if cls in spec.applies_to]


REGISTRY = Registry(SPECS)
```

Add `Tier` and `Direction` to the imports even though this task's rows use the defaults — Slice 5 needs them and leaving the import in avoids a spurious diff later. (If ruff flags them as unused, add the rows that need them in Slice 5 and let ruff pass now by omitting them; either is fine.)

### Task 3.5: The golden-vector fixture and the flat `set()` API

**Files:**
- Create: `tests/fixtures/golden_params.yaml`
- Create: `tests/test_golden_vectors.py`
- Modify: `qumidi/client_async.py` (`set()`, `_encode_param`)
- Modify: `qumidi/cli.py` (`set` handles registry parameters)
- Modify: `qumidi/testing/fake_qu.py` (`_push_state` emits a canned NRPN burst)
- Test: `tests/test_set_param.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `REGISTRY`, `encode_nrpn`, `send_target_vx`.
- Produces:
  - `await mixer.set(ch: ChannelId, param: str, *, raw: int | None = None, db: float | None = None, vx: ChannelId | int | None = None) -> None`
  - `mixer.encode(ch, param, raw, vx) -> bytes` (public, so the golden-vector test and the CLI can both use it without a socket)
  - `FakeQu.state: dict[tuple[int,int,int], int]` keyed `(ch, param_id, vx)`, and a `scene` seed pushed at sync.
  - `qumidi set --host H fader input:6 -6db` and `... 0x62` / `... raw:98`.

**The fixture is transcribed independently from the PDF, never generated from the registry (PRD §13.2).** A fixture generated from the code under test proves nothing. Its whole purpose is to catch a wrong `VX` filler or a transposed NRPN id — the failure modes that produce silently-ignored messages. Slice 5 extends it to every parameter and adds the completeness assertion.

Parsing YAML without a dependency: the fixture uses a deliberately flat shape and `tests/test_golden_vectors.py` includes a ~30-line reader for it. Do **not** add PyYAML — PRD §2 forbids runtime deps and this file must stay readable by hand anyway.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the golden-vector fixture**

`tests/fixtures/golden_params.yaml` — transcribe each line from the PRD §7.2 tables by hand, checking the `VX` column character by character:

```yaml
# Golden vectors: expected wire bytes for registry parameters.
#
# TRANSCRIBED BY HAND FROM THE PRD SECTION 7.2 TABLES. Never regenerate this
# file from the registry -- a fixture generated from the code under test
# proves nothing. Its purpose is to catch a wrong VX filler byte or a
# transposed NRPN id, which produce messages the mixer silently ignores.
#
# fields: param, channel (hex CH), value (hex), vx (hex or null),
#         midi_channel (default 0), expected (space-separated hex bytes)

- param: fader
  channel: 0x25        # Input 6
  value: 0x62          # 0 dB
  vx: null
  expected: "B0 63 25 B0 62 17 B0 06 62 B0 26 07"

- param: fader
  channel: 0x25
  value: 0x00          # -inf
  vx: null
  expected: "B0 63 25 B0 62 17 B0 06 00 B0 26 07"

- param: fader
  channel: 0x67        # LR
  value: 0x7F          # +10 dB
  vx: null
  expected: "B0 63 67 B0 62 17 B0 06 7F B0 26 07"

- param: fader
  channel: 0x25
  value: 0x62
  vx: null
  midi_channel: 5      # non-zero N: B0 is the easy case, B5 is where a
  expected: "B5 63 25 B5 62 17 B5 06 62 B5 26 07"   # hardcoded zero shows up

- param: send_level
  channel: 0x25
  value: 0x50
  vx: 0x02             # Mix 3
  expected: "B0 63 25 B0 62 20 B0 06 50 B0 26 02"

- param: send_level
  channel: 0x25
  value: 0x62
  vx: 0x13             # FX Send 4 -- the top of the non-dense target table
  expected: "B0 63 25 B0 62 20 B0 06 62 B0 26 13"

- param: pan
  channel: 0x25
  value: 0x00          # full left
  vx: 0x07             # LR
  expected: "B0 63 25 B0 62 16 B0 06 00 B0 26 07"

- param: pan
  channel: 0x25
  value: 0x25          # centre -- NOT 0x40 (PRD 14.9)
  vx: 0x07
  expected: "B0 63 25 B0 62 16 B0 06 25 B0 26 07"

- param: pan
  channel: 0x25
  value: 0x4A          # full right -- the ceiling, not 0x7F
  vx: 0x07
  expected: "B0 63 25 B0 62 16 B0 06 4A B0 26 07"

- param: lr_assign
  channel: 0x25
  value: 0x01
  vx: null
  expected: "B0 63 25 B0 62 18 B0 06 01 B0 26 07"

- param: mix_assign
  channel: 0x25
  value: 0x01
  vx: 0x02             # Mix 3
  expected: "B0 63 25 B0 62 55 B0 06 01 B0 26 02"

- param: mix_assign
  channel: 0x25
  value: 0x01
  vx: 0x08             # Group 1-2
  expected: "B0 63 25 B0 62 55 B0 06 01 B0 26 08"

- param: mix_pre
  channel: 0x25
  value: 0x01          # pre
  vx: 0x00             # Mix 1
  expected: "B0 63 25 B0 62 50 B0 06 01 B0 26 00"

- param: pafl
  channel: 0x25
  value: 0x01
  vx: null
  expected: "B0 63 25 B0 62 51 B0 06 01 B0 26 07"
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the failing golden-vector test**

`tests/test_golden_vectors.py`:

```python
"""The highest-value test set (PRD 13.2).

These vectors are transcribed by hand from the spec, not generated from the
registry. They exist to catch a wrong VX filler byte or a transposed NRPN id,
which produce messages the mixer silently ignores -- the single hardest
failure mode to debug in the field.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import pathlib

import pytest

from qumidi.midi.nrpn import encode_nrpn
from qumidi.protocol.channels import channel_from_raw
from qumidi.protocol.registry import REGISTRY

FIXTURE = pathlib.Path(__file__).parent / "fixtures" / "golden_params.yaml"


def load_vectors(path: pathlib.Path) -> list[dict[str, object]]:
    """Read the deliberately flat fixture format without a YAML dependency."""
    vectors: list[dict[str, object]] = []
    current: dict[str, object] | None = None
    for line in path.read_text().splitlines():
        stripped = line.split("#", 1)[0].rstrip()
        if not stripped.strip():
            continue
        if stripped.startswith("- "):
            current = {}
            vectors.append(current)
            stripped = "  " + stripped[2:]
        assert current is not None, f"fixture line outside an entry: {line!r}"
        key, _, raw = stripped.strip().partition(":")
        value = raw.strip()
        if value.startswith('"') and value.endswith('"'):
            current[key] = value[1:-1]
        elif value == "null":
            current[key] = None
        else:
            current[key] = int(value, 0)
    return vectors


VECTORS = load_vectors(FIXTURE)


def vector_id(vec: dict[str, object]) -> str:
    return f"{vec['param']}-ch{vec['channel']:#04x}-v{vec['value']:#04x}-vx{vec['vx']}"  # type: ignore[str-format]


def spec_for(param: str, cls):  # type: ignore[no-untyped-def]
    """Resolve a vector's ParamSpec, tolerating console-global parameters.

    group_mode has no documented CH (PRD 7.2), so its vector carries a
    placeholder channel byte whose class the spec does not claim. Fall back to
    a class-free lookup for those rather than special-casing the fixture.
    """
    from qumidi.errors import UnknownParameterError

    try:
        return REGISTRY.lookup(param, cls)
    except UnknownParameterError:
        return REGISTRY.lookup(param)


@pytest.mark.parametrize("vec", VECTORS, ids=vector_id)
def test_golden_vector_encodes_to_the_expected_bytes(vec: dict[str, object]) -> None:
    ch = channel_from_raw(int(vec["channel"]))  # type: ignore[arg-type]
    spec = spec_for(str(vec["param"]), ch.cls)
    vx = spec.vx.validate(vec["vx"], spec.name)  # type: ignore[arg-type]
    got = encode_nrpn(
        midi_channel=int(vec.get("midi_channel", 0)),  # type: ignore[arg-type]
        ch=ch.value,
        param_id=spec.nrpn_id,
        value=int(vec["value"]),  # type: ignore[arg-type]
        vx=vx,
    )
    expected = bytes.fromhex(str(vec["expected"]).replace(" ", ""))
    assert got == expected, (
        f"{spec.name}: encoded {got.hex(' ')} but the spec says {expected.hex(' ')}"
    )


@pytest.mark.parametrize("vec", VECTORS, ids=vector_id)
def test_golden_vector_parses_back_to_the_same_logical_value(vec: dict[str, object]) -> None:
    # PRD 16.2: set via API -> observed bytes match the vector -> parsed back
    # to the same logical value.
    from qumidi.midi.nrpn import NrpnDecoder
    from qumidi.midi.parser import MidiStreamParser

    expected = bytes.fromhex(str(vec["expected"]).replace(" ", ""))
    decoder = NrpnDecoder()
    updates = []
    for msg in MidiStreamParser().feed(expected):
        updates.extend(decoder.feed(msg))
    updates.extend(decoder.flush())
    assert len(updates) == 1
    update = updates[0]
    ch = channel_from_raw(int(vec["channel"]))  # type: ignore[arg-type]
    spec = spec_for(str(vec["param"]), ch.cls)
    assert update.ch == ch.value
    assert update.param_id == spec.nrpn_id
    assert update.value == int(vec["value"])  # type: ignore[arg-type]
    assert REGISTRY.resolve(ch.cls, update.param_id, update.vx) is spec


def test_the_fixture_actually_loaded() -> None:
    assert len(VECTORS) >= 14
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write the failing tests for `set()`**

`tests/test_set_param.py`:

```python
import asyncio

import pytest

from qumidi.client_async import AsyncQuMixer
from qumidi.errors import (
    NotSyncedError,
    ParameterRangeError,
    UnknownParameterError,
    UnsupportedOnModelError,
)
from qumidi.protocol.channels import Group, Input, Mix


async def test_set_fader_by_raw_emits_the_golden_bytes(fake_qu_factory) -> None:  # type: ignore[no-untyped-def]
    fake = await fake_qu_factory()
    async with AsyncQuMixer("127.0.0.1", port=fake.port) as mixer:
        await mixer.wait_synced()
        fake.received.clear()
        await mixer.set(Input(6), "fader", raw=0x62)
        await asyncio.sleep(0.05)
    assert bytes.fromhex("B063 25 B062 17 B006 62 B026 07".replace(" ", "")) in bytes(
        fake.received
    )


async def test_set_fader_by_db(fake_qu_factory) -> None:  # type: ignore[no-untyped-def]
    fake = await fake_qu_factory()
    async with AsyncQuMixer("127.0.0.1", port=fake.port) as mixer:
        await mixer.wait_synced()
        fake.received.clear()
        await mixer.set(Input(6), "fader", db=-10.0)
        await asyncio.sleep(0.05)
        assert fake.state[(0x25, 0x17, 0x07)] == 0x3F


async def test_set_send_level_takes_a_channel_id_as_vx(fake_qu_factory) -> None:  # type: ignore[no-untyped-def]
    fake = await fake_qu_factory()
    async with AsyncQuMixer("127.0.0.1", port=fake.port) as mixer:
        await mixer.wait_synced()
        await mixer.set(Input(6), "send_level", raw=0x50, vx=Mix.MIX3)
        await asyncio.sleep(0.05)
        assert fake.state[(0x25, 0x20, 0x02)] == 0x50


async def test_set_rejects_a_value_outside_the_domain(fake_qu_factory) -> None:  # type: ignore[no-untyped-def]
    fake = await fake_qu_factory()
    async with AsyncQuMixer("127.0.0.1", port=fake.port) as mixer:
        await mixer.wait_synced()
        with pytest.raises(ParameterRangeError) as excinfo:
            await mixer.set(Input(6), "pan", raw=0x7F, vx=Mix.LR)
        assert "Input 6" in str(excinfo.value)
        assert "pan" in str(excinfo.value)


async def test_mix_assign_to_lr_is_rejected(fake_qu_factory) -> None:  # type: ignore[no-untyped-def]
    # PRD 14.8: encode LR assignment exclusively as 18; 55/07 is rejected.
    fake = await fake_qu_factory()
    async with AsyncQuMixer("127.0.0.1", port=fake.port) as mixer:
        await mixer.wait_synced()
        with pytest.raises(ParameterRangeError) as excinfo:
            await mixer.set(Input(6), "mix_assign", raw=0x01, vx=Mix.LR)
        assert "lr_assign" in str(excinfo.value)


async def test_send_to_a_group_is_rejected_on_qu16(fake_qu_factory) -> None:  # type: ignore[no-untyped-def]
    fake = await fake_qu_factory(model="qu16")
    async with AsyncQuMixer("127.0.0.1", port=fake.port) as mixer:
        await mixer.wait_synced()
        with pytest.raises(UnsupportedOnModelError):
            await mixer.set(Input(6), "send_level", raw=0x50, vx=Group(1))


async def test_set_an_unknown_parameter_raises(fake_qu_factory) -> None:  # type: ignore[no-untyped-def]
    fake = await fake_qu_factory()
    async with AsyncQuMixer("127.0.0.1", port=fake.port) as mixer:
        await mixer.wait_synced()
        with pytest.raises(UnknownParameterError):
            await mixer.set(Input(6), "volume", raw=0x62)


async def test_set_requires_exactly_one_of_raw_and_db(fake_qu_factory) -> None:  # type: ignore[no-untyped-def]
    fake = await fake_qu_factory()
    async with AsyncQuMixer("127.0.0.1", port=fake.port) as mixer:
        await mixer.wait_synced()
        with pytest.raises(ValueError):
            await mixer.set(Input(6), "fader")
        with pytest.raises(ValueError):
            await mixer.set(Input(6), "fader", raw=0x62, db=0.0)


async def test_db_on_a_parameter_with_no_taper_raises(fake_qu_factory) -> None:  # type: ignore[no-untyped-def]
    fake = await fake_qu_factory()
    async with AsyncQuMixer("127.0.0.1", port=fake.port) as mixer:
        await mixer.wait_synced()
        with pytest.raises(ParameterRangeError):
            await mixer.set(Input(6), "lr_assign", db=0.0)


async def test_set_before_sync_raises_not_synced() -> None:
    mixer = AsyncQuMixer("127.0.0.1", port=1)
    with pytest.raises(NotSyncedError):
        await mixer.set(Input(6), "fader", raw=0x62)


async def test_the_state_burst_arrives_before_the_end_marker(fake_qu_factory) -> None:  # type: ignore[no-untyped-def]
    fake = await fake_qu_factory(scene={(0x25, 0x17, 0x07): 0x4F})
    async with AsyncQuMixer("127.0.0.1", port=fake.port) as mixer:
        await mixer.wait_synced()

        # burst is emitted and parsed without breaking the sync handshake.
        assert mixer.state.name == "READY"
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Implement `set()` in `AsyncQuMixer`**

```python
    def encode(
        self,
        ch: ChannelId,
        param: str,
        raw: int,
        vx: ChannelId | int | None = None,
    ) -> bytes:
        """Build the wire bytes for one parameter write, without sending.

        Public so that the golden-vector tests and the CLI can exercise the
        encoder without a socket.
        """
        n = self._require_synced(f"encode {param} for {ch.label}")
        spec = REGISTRY.lookup(param, ch.cls)
        if spec.direction is Direction.RX_ONLY:
            raise ReadOnlyParameterError(
                f"{ch.label} {spec.name} is receive-only ({spec.notes or 'PRD 7.1'}); "
                f"writing it would emit bytes the mixer ignores"
            )
        check_channel(self._caps, ch)
        vx_byte = self._resolve_vx(spec, vx)
        value = spec.domain.validate_raw(raw, spec.name, ch.label)
        return encode_nrpn(n, ch=ch.value, param_id=spec.nrpn_id, value=value, vx=vx_byte)

    def _resolve_vx(self, spec: ParamSpec, vx: ChannelId | int | None) -> int:
        if isinstance(spec.vx, SendTarget):
            if isinstance(vx, ChannelId):
                target_vx = send_target_vx(vx, self._caps)
            elif isinstance(vx, int):
                target_vx = vx
            else:
                raise ParameterRangeError(
                    f"{spec.name} needs a send destination; pass vx=Mix.MIX3, "
                    f"vx=Group(1), vx=Mix.LR and so on"
                )
            if spec.name == "mix_assign" and target_vx == 0x07:
                # PRD 14.8: 55/07 is a syntactically valid second encoding for
                # LR assignment, but only one is what the console listens to.
                raise ParameterRangeError(
                    "mix_assign to LR is ambiguous with lr_assign (PRD 14.8); "
                    "use set(ch, 'lr_assign', ...) instead"
                )
            return spec.vx.validate(target_vx, spec.name)
        if isinstance(vx, ChannelId):
            raise ParameterRangeError(
                f"{spec.name} does not take a send destination; its VX is not a target"
            )
        return spec.vx.validate(vx, spec.name)

    async def set(
        self,
        ch: ChannelId,
        param: str,
        *,
        raw: int | None = None,
        db: float | None = None,
        vx: ChannelId | int | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        """Write one parameter. Resolves once the bytes have been drained."""
        if (raw is None) == (db is None):
            raise ValueError("pass exactly one of raw= or db=")
        spec = REGISTRY.lookup(param, ch.cls)
        if db is not None:
            if not isinstance(spec.domain, Interpolated):
                raise ParameterRangeError(
                    f"{ch.label} {spec.name} has no taper; pass raw= instead of db="
                )
            raw = spec.domain.to_raw(db)
        assert raw is not None
        # PRD 11.1: a write issued while a connect is in flight waits here for
        # the 11 reply rather than being refused.
        await self._await_channel(f"set {param} on {ch.label}")
        await self._send(self.encode(ch, param, raw, vx))
```

Imports to add to `client_async.py`: `from qumidi.midi.nrpn import encode_nrpn`, `from qumidi.protocol.params import Direction, Interpolated, ParamSpec, SendTarget`, `from qumidi.protocol.registry import REGISTRY, send_target_vx`, and `ParameterRangeError, ReadOnlyParameterError` from `qumidi.errors`.

# Slice 4 — It knows what the console is doing

**Slice goal:** `qumidi monitor --host H` prints a line every time you touch the console, and does *not* print a line for writes the library itself made. This is the slice that makes the library a state mirror rather than a remote control.

**The bug this slice exists to avoid (PRD §10.1a, §14.10).** The obvious cache key `(ChannelId, param, vx)` collapses all four DCA memberships of a channel into one entry, because their `VX` is the constant `0x07` and the group index lives in the **value**. Input 6 assigned to DCA 1 *and* DCA 3 is two independent facts; a single-slot cache reports the second write as having turned the first off. The same bug applies to mute-group assign. This is a functional bug, not a documentation gap, and it is the single easiest thing to get wrong in the whole spec.

---

### Task 4.1: The state cache

**Files:**
- Create: `qumidi/state.py`
- Test: `tests/test_state_cache.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `ChannelId`, `params`.
- Produces:
  - `Freshness` enum: `UNKNOWN, KNOWN, UNCONFIRMED, STALE`.
  - `CachedValue` frozen dataclass: `raw: int | None`, `freshness: Freshness`, `updated_at: float | None`.
  - `UNKNOWN_VALUE: CachedValue` singleton (`raw=None, freshness=UNKNOWN, updated_at=None`).
  - `CacheKey = tuple[int, ChannelClass, str, int | None]`.
  - `StateCache(now=time.monotonic)` with `get(ch, param, sub=None)`, `set(ch, param, sub, raw, freshness) -> tuple[CachedValue, CachedValue]`, `mark_all_stale()`, `clear()`, `keys()`, `items()`, `membership(ch, param, count) -> MembershipSet`.
  - `MembershipSet` frozen dataclass: `.known: frozenset[int]`, `.unknown: frozenset[int]`, `.complete: bool`, `.require() -> frozenset[int]` (raises `ValueUnknownError` if incomplete).
  - `sub_index_for(spec, vx, value) -> int | None` — the one function implementing the PRD §10.1a table.

**The sub-index table (PRD §10.1a), implemented once:**

| Parameter shape | `sub_index` | Example |
|---|---|---|
| `Fixed` filler `VX` | `None` | fader, HPF freq |
| `SendTarget` / `GeqBand` / `DsnakeSocket` / `FxTap` | the `VX` byte | send level to Mix 3 → `0x02` |
| `FlaggedIndex` domain | **the low bits of the value**, not `VX` | DCA assign, mute group assign |

`tests/test_state_cache.py`:

```python
import pytest

from qumidi.errors import ValueUnknownError
from qumidi.protocol.channels import Input, Mix
from qumidi.protocol.params import FlaggedIndex, Fixed, GeqBand, SendTarget
from qumidi.protocol.registry import REGISTRY
from qumidi.state import (
    UNKNOWN_VALUE,
    CachedValue,
    Freshness,
    StateCache,
    sub_index_for,
)


def clock():  # type: ignore[no-untyped-def]
    t = {"now": 1000.0}
    def now() -> float:
        return t["now"]
    return t, now


def test_an_unseen_value_is_unknown_not_a_default() -> None:
    # PRD 10.1: "I have not been told the value" is categorically different
    # from "the fader is at -inf". Never synthesize defaults.
    cache = StateCache()
    got = cache.get(Input(6), "fader")
    assert got == UNKNOWN_VALUE
    assert got.raw is None
    assert got.freshness is Freshness.UNKNOWN


def test_set_and_get_round_trip_with_a_timestamp() -> None:
    t, now = clock()
    cache = StateCache(now=now)
    old, new = cache.set(Input(6), "fader", None, 0x62, Freshness.KNOWN)
    assert old is UNKNOWN_VALUE
    assert new == CachedValue(raw=0x62, freshness=Freshness.KNOWN, updated_at=1000.0)
    assert cache.get(Input(6), "fader") == new


def test_the_clock_is_monotonic_not_wall_clock() -> None:
    # PRD 10.1: echo windows and staleness must not break when NTP steps the
    # system clock.
    import time

    cache = StateCache()
    _, new = cache.set(Input(6), "fader", None, 0x62, Freshness.KNOWN)
    assert new.updated_at is not None
    assert abs(new.updated_at - time.monotonic()) < 1.0


def test_send_level_to_different_mixes_are_independent_entries() -> None:
    cache = StateCache()
    cache.set(Input(6), "send_level", 0x02, 0x50, Freshness.KNOWN)
    cache.set(Input(6), "send_level", 0x03, 0x20, Freshness.KNOWN)
    assert cache.get(Input(6), "send_level", 0x02).raw == 0x50
    assert cache.get(Input(6), "send_level", 0x03).raw == 0x20


def test_the_same_param_on_different_channels_is_independent() -> None:
    cache = StateCache()
    cache.set(Input(6), "fader", None, 0x62, Freshness.KNOWN)
    cache.set(Mix.MIX3, "fader", None, 0x10, Freshness.KNOWN)
    assert cache.get(Input(6), "fader").raw == 0x62
    assert cache.get(Mix.MIX3, "fader").raw == 0x10


def test_mark_all_stale_preserves_the_values_but_downgrades_freshness() -> None:
    # PRD 4.4: never serve pre-disconnect values as current.
    cache = StateCache()
    cache.set(Input(6), "fader", None, 0x62, Freshness.KNOWN)
    cache.mark_all_stale()
    got = cache.get(Input(6), "fader")
    assert got.raw == 0x62
    assert got.freshness is Freshness.STALE


def test_clear_forgets_everything() -> None:
    cache = StateCache()
    cache.set(Input(6), "fader", None, 0x62, Freshness.KNOWN)
    cache.clear()
    assert cache.get(Input(6), "fader") is UNKNOWN_VALUE


# --- the PRD 10.1a sub-index rule ---------------------------------------


def test_fixed_filler_vx_contributes_no_sub_index() -> None:
    spec = REGISTRY.lookup("fader")
    assert isinstance(spec.vx, Fixed)
    assert sub_index_for(spec, vx=0x07, value=0x62) is None


def test_send_target_vx_is_the_sub_index() -> None:
    spec = REGISTRY.lookup("send_level")
    assert isinstance(spec.vx, SendTarget)
    assert sub_index_for(spec, vx=0x02, value=0x50) == 0x02


def test_flagged_index_takes_its_sub_index_from_the_value_not_vx() -> None:
    # PRD 14.10: this is the correction. The naive key loses three quarters
    # of the information.
    from qumidi.protocol.params import ParamSpec
    from qumidi.protocol.channels import ChannelClass

    spec = ParamSpec(
        name="dca_assign",
        nrpn_id=0x40,
        vx=Fixed(0x07),
        domain=FlaggedIndex(on_flag=0x40, count=4),
        applies_to=frozenset({ChannelClass.INPUT}),
    )
    assert sub_index_for(spec, vx=0x07, value=0x40) == 0
    assert sub_index_for(spec, vx=0x07, value=0x42) == 2
    assert sub_index_for(spec, vx=0x07, value=0x02) == 2


def test_two_dca_memberships_are_independently_known() -> None:
    # PRD 16.10 and the whole reason 10.1a was rewritten: assigning one input
    # to DCA 1 and DCA 3 must leave both readable, and neither write must
    # clear the other.
    cache = StateCache()
    cache.set(Input(6), "dca_assign", 0, 0x40, Freshness.KNOWN)
    cache.set(Input(6), "dca_assign", 2, 0x42, Freshness.KNOWN)
    assert cache.get(Input(6), "dca_assign", 0).raw == 0x40
    assert cache.get(Input(6), "dca_assign", 2).raw == 0x42


def test_membership_reports_partial_knowledge_rather_than_fabricating_a_set() -> None:
    # PRD 10.1a: if DCA 1 is KNOWN and 2-4 are UNKNOWN, the read must never
    # return {1} as though 2-4 were known to be off.
    cache = StateCache()
    cache.set(Input(6), "dca_assign", 0, 0x40, Freshness.KNOWN)
    membership = cache.membership(Input(6), "dca_assign", count=4, on_flag=0x40)
    assert membership.known == frozenset({0})
    assert membership.unknown == frozenset({1, 2, 3})
    assert membership.complete is False
    with pytest.raises(ValueUnknownError) as excinfo:
        membership.require()
    assert "dca_assign" in str(excinfo.value)


def test_membership_returns_a_set_once_every_bit_is_known() -> None:
    cache = StateCache()
    cache.set(Input(6), "dca_assign", 0, 0x40, Freshness.KNOWN)
    cache.set(Input(6), "dca_assign", 1, 0x01, Freshness.KNOWN)
    cache.set(Input(6), "dca_assign", 2, 0x42, Freshness.KNOWN)
    cache.set(Input(6), "dca_assign", 3, 0x03, Freshness.KNOWN)
    membership = cache.membership(Input(6), "dca_assign", count=4, on_flag=0x40)
    assert membership.complete is True
    assert membership.require() == frozenset({0, 2})


def test_items_enumerates_every_entry_for_snapshotting() -> None:
    cache = StateCache()
    cache.set(Input(6), "fader", None, 0x62, Freshness.KNOWN)
    cache.set(Input(6), "send_level", 0x02, 0x50, Freshness.UNCONFIRMED)
    assert len(list(cache.items())) == 2
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `qumidi/state.py`**

```python
"""The local state cache (PRD 10.1, 10.1a).

Never synthesize defaults. "I have not been told the value" is categorically
different from "the fader is at -inf", and conflating them will cause a user
to unmute something they shouldn't.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import time
from collections.abc import Callable, Iterator
from dataclasses import dataclass
from enum import Enum, auto

from qumidi.errors import ValueUnknownError
from qumidi.protocol.channels import ChannelClass, ChannelId
from qumidi.protocol.params import FlaggedIndex, ParamSpec, VxMode


class Freshness(Enum):
    """Four states, not three (PRD 10.1)."""

    #: Never received.
    UNKNOWN = auto()
    #: Confirmed by the mixer.
    KNOWN = auto()
    #: We wrote it; the mixer never confirmed it. The only signal a user gets
    #: that the library sent bytes into the void (PRD 10.3).
    UNCONFIRMED = auto()
    #: Was known; the connection has dropped since.
    STALE = auto()


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class CachedValue:
    raw: int | None
    freshness: Freshness
    #: A time.monotonic() reading, never wall clock (PRD 10.1).
    updated_at: float | None


UNKNOWN_VALUE = CachedValue(raw=None, freshness=Freshness.UNKNOWN, updated_at=None)

#: (channel wire value, channel class, param name, sub index).
#: The class is part of the key so the synthetic GLOBAL and DSNAKE channels,
#: which share the wire value -1, cannot collide.
CacheKey = tuple[int, ChannelClass, str, "int | None"]


def sub_index_for(spec: ParamSpec, vx: int | None, value: int) -> int | None:
    """The parameter's *addressing* index (PRD 10.1a).

    This is not simply ``vx``. It comes from one of three places depending on
    the VxMode/Domain -- see the table in PRD 10.1a. The third row is the
    correction that PRD revision 1.1 exists for.
    """
    if isinstance(spec.domain, FlaggedIndex):
        return spec.domain.sub_index_from_value(value)
    if vx is None:
        return None
    return _vx_sub_index(spec.vx, vx, value)


def _vx_sub_index(mode: VxMode, vx: int, value: int) -> int | None:
    return mode.sub_index(vx, value)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class MembershipSet:
    """The result of reading a set-valued parameter (PRD 10.1a).

    A cache read must not fabricate a set from partial knowledge: if DCA 1 is
    KNOWN and 2-4 are UNKNOWN, this reports that rather than returning {1}.
    """

    param: str
    channel_label: str
    known_on: frozenset[int]
    known_off: frozenset[int]
    unknown: frozenset[int]

    @property
    def known(self) -> frozenset[int]:
        return self.known_on

    @property
    def complete(self) -> bool:
        return not self.unknown

    def require(self) -> frozenset[int]:
        """Return the membership set, or explain what is missing."""
        if self.unknown:
            missing = ", ".join(str(i + 1) for i in sorted(self.unknown))
            raise ValueUnknownError(
                f"{self.channel_label} {self.param}: membership of group(s) {missing} has "
                f"never been reported by the console, so the set is not knowable. "
                f"Groups known on: {sorted(i + 1 for i in self.known_on) or 'none'}."
            )
        return self.known_on


class StateCache:
    """A dict of CachedValue keyed by (channel, param, sub index)."""

    def __init__(self, now: Callable[[], float] = time.monotonic) -> None:
        self._now = now
        self._values: dict[CacheKey, CachedValue] = {}

    @staticmethod
    def key(ch: ChannelId, param: str, sub: int | None) -> CacheKey:
        return (ch.value if ch.cls not in (ChannelClass.GLOBAL, ChannelClass.DSNAKE)
                else ch.index, ch.cls, param, sub)

    def get(self, ch: ChannelId, param: str, sub: int | None = None) -> CachedValue:
        return self._values.get(self.key(ch, param, sub), UNKNOWN_VALUE)

    def set(
        self,
        ch: ChannelId,
        param: str,
        sub: int | None,
        raw: int,
        freshness: Freshness,
    ) -> tuple[CachedValue, CachedValue]:
        """Store a value, returning ``(old, new)``."""
        key = self.key(ch, param, sub)
        old = self._values.get(key, UNKNOWN_VALUE)
        new = CachedValue(raw=raw, freshness=freshness, updated_at=self._now())
        self._values[key] = new
        return old, new

    def downgrade(self, ch: ChannelId, param: str, sub: int | None, freshness: Freshness) -> None:
        """Change only the freshness of an existing entry (PRD 10.3)."""
        key = self.key(ch, param, sub)
        current = self._values.get(key)
        if current is None:
            return
        self._values[key] = CachedValue(
            raw=current.raw, freshness=freshness, updated_at=current.updated_at
        )

    def mark_all_stale(self) -> None:
        """PRD 4.4: on every reconnect, before re-running the full sync."""
        for key, value in list(self._values.items()):
            if value.freshness is not Freshness.UNKNOWN:
                self._values[key] = CachedValue(
                    raw=value.raw, freshness=Freshness.STALE, updated_at=value.updated_at
                )

    def clear(self) -> None:
        self._values.clear()

    def items(self) -> Iterator[tuple[CacheKey, CachedValue]]:
        return iter(self._values.items())

    def __len__(self) -> int:
        return len(self._values)

    def membership(
        self, ch: ChannelId, param: str, count: int, on_flag: int = 0x40
    ) -> MembershipSet:
        """Read a FlaggedIndex parameter as a set, honestly (PRD 10.1a)."""
        on: set[int] = set()
        off: set[int] = set()
        unknown: set[int] = set()
        for index in range(count):
            value = self.get(ch, param, index)
            if value.raw is None or value.freshness is Freshness.UNKNOWN:
                unknown.add(index)
            elif value.raw & on_flag:
                on.add(index)
            else:
                off.add(index)
        return MembershipSet(
            param=param,
            channel_label=ch.label,
            known_on=frozenset(on),
            known_off=frozenset(off),
            unknown=frozenset(unknown),
        )
```

### Task 4.2: The event bus

**Files:**
- Create: `qumidi/events.py`
- Test: `tests/test_events.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `state`, `channels`, `constants`.
- Produces:
  - `Source` enum: `LOCAL, MIXER, SYNC`.
  - `ChangeEvent` frozen dataclass: `channel, param, sub_index, old, new, source`.
  - `EventBus(slow_callback_warn=0.1)` with `on_change(cb, channel=None, param=None) -> Callable[[], None]` (the return value unsubscribes), `on_connection_state(cb)`, `on_sync_complete(cb)`, `on_write_unconfirmed(cb)`, `on_scene_recalled(cb)`, `on_meter_layout_error(cb)`, and matching `emit_*` methods.

**Two rules (PRD §10.4):** exceptions in user callbacks are caught and logged, never allowed to kill the read loop. And a slow callback blocks the read loop — log a warning when one exceeds `slow_callback_warn` (100 ms).

`tests/test_events.py`:

```python
import logging

import pytest

from qumidi.events import ChangeEvent, EventBus, Source
from qumidi.protocol.channels import Input, Mix
from qumidi.state import CachedValue, Freshness

KNOWN = CachedValue(raw=0x62, freshness=Freshness.KNOWN, updated_at=1.0)
OLD = CachedValue(raw=0x50, freshness=Freshness.KNOWN, updated_at=0.0)


def event(channel=Input(6), param="fader", sub=None, source=Source.MIXER) -> ChangeEvent:  # type: ignore[no-untyped-def]
    return ChangeEvent(channel=channel, param=param, sub_index=sub, old=OLD, new=KNOWN,
                       source=source)


def test_unfiltered_subscription_receives_everything() -> None:
    bus = EventBus()
    seen: list[ChangeEvent] = []
    bus.on_change(seen.append)
    bus.emit_change(event())
    bus.emit_change(event(channel=Mix.MIX3, param="pafl"))
    assert len(seen) == 2


def test_channel_filter() -> None:
    bus = EventBus()
    seen: list[ChangeEvent] = []
    bus.on_change(seen.append, channel=Input(6))
    bus.emit_change(event(channel=Input(6)))
    bus.emit_change(event(channel=Input(7)))
    assert [e.channel for e in seen] == [Input(6)]


def test_param_filter() -> None:
    bus = EventBus()
    seen: list[ChangeEvent] = []
    bus.on_change(seen.append, param="fader")
    bus.emit_change(event(param="fader"))
    bus.emit_change(event(param="pafl"))
    assert [e.param for e in seen] == ["fader"]


def test_unsubscribe() -> None:
    bus = EventBus()
    seen: list[ChangeEvent] = []
    unsubscribe = bus.on_change(seen.append)
    bus.emit_change(event())
    unsubscribe()
    bus.emit_change(event())
    assert len(seen) == 1


def test_a_raising_callback_does_not_stop_the_others(caplog) -> None:  # type: ignore[no-untyped-def]
    # PRD 10.4: exceptions in user callbacks are caught and logged, never
    # allowed to kill the read loop.
    bus = EventBus()
    seen: list[ChangeEvent] = []

    def boom(_: ChangeEvent) -> None:
        raise RuntimeError("user code")

    bus.on_change(boom)
    bus.on_change(seen.append)
    with caplog.at_level(logging.ERROR):
        bus.emit_change(event())
    assert len(seen) == 1
    assert any("user code" in r.message or r.exc_info for r in caplog.records)


def test_a_slow_callback_logs_a_warning(caplog) -> None:  # type: ignore[no-untyped-def]
    ticks = iter([0.0, 0.5])
    bus = EventBus(slow_callback_warn=0.1, now=lambda: next(ticks))
    bus.on_change(lambda _: None)
    with caplog.at_level(logging.WARNING):
        bus.emit_change(event())
    assert any("slow" in r.message.lower() for r in caplog.records)


def test_the_other_subscription_kinds_fire() -> None:
    from qumidi.transport.base import ConnectionState

    bus = EventBus()
    calls: dict[str, object] = {}
    bus.on_connection_state(lambda s: calls.__setitem__("state", s))
    bus.on_sync_complete(lambda: calls.__setitem__("sync", True))
    bus.on_write_unconfirmed(lambda ch, p, s: calls.__setitem__("unconfirmed", (ch, p, s)))
    bus.on_scene_recalled(lambda n: calls.__setitem__("scene", n))
    bus.emit_connection_state(ConnectionState.READY)
    bus.emit_sync_complete()
    bus.emit_write_unconfirmed(Input(6), "fader", None)
    bus.emit_scene_recalled(12)
    assert calls == {
        "state": ConnectionState.READY,
        "sync": True,
        "unconfirmed": (Input(6), "fader", None),
        "scene": 12,
    }


def test_change_event_carries_the_sub_index() -> None:
    # PRD 10.4: the payload includes the sub-index from 10.1a.
    e = event(param="dca_assign", sub=2)
    assert e.sub_index == 2
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `qumidi/events.py`**

```python
"""Change notification (PRD 10.4).

Callbacks are dispatched synchronously on the event loop. Exceptions in user
callbacks are caught and logged, never allowed to kill the read loop, and a
callback that blocks for too long is reported -- it is holding up inbound
MIDI while it runs.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import logging
import time
from collections.abc import Callable
from dataclasses import dataclass
from enum import Enum, auto
from typing import Any

from qumidi.protocol import constants as c
from qumidi.protocol.channels import ChannelId
from qumidi.state import CachedValue
from qumidi.transport.base import ConnectionState

log = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class Source(Enum):
    #: This client wrote it.
    LOCAL = auto()
    #: The console reported it outside a sync burst.
    MIXER = auto()
    #: Part of a state-sync burst; no external_change is emitted for these.
    SYNC = auto()


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ChangeEvent:
    channel: ChannelId
    param: str
    sub_index: int | None
    old: CachedValue
    new: CachedValue
    source: Source


@dataclass
class _Subscription:
    callback: Callable[[ChangeEvent], None]
    channel: ChannelId | None
    param: str | None

    def matches(self, event: ChangeEvent) -> bool:
        if self.channel is not None and self.channel != event.channel:
            return False
        if self.param is not None and self.param != event.param:
            return False
        return True


class EventBus:
    """Filtered subscriptions over the client's state changes."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        slow_callback_warn: float = c.SLOW_CALLBACK_WARN,
        now: Callable[[], float] = time.monotonic,
    ) -> None:
        self.slow_callback_warn = slow_callback_warn
        self._now = now
        self._changes: list[_Subscription] = []
        self._connection: list[Callable[[ConnectionState], None]] = []
        self._sync: list[Callable[[], None]] = []
        self._unconfirmed: list[Callable[[ChannelId, str, int | None], None]] = []
        self._scene: list[Callable[[int], None]] = []
        self._meter_layout: list[Callable[[str], None]] = []

    # -- subscription -----------------------------------------------------

    def on_change(
        self,
        callback: Callable[[ChangeEvent], None],
        channel: ChannelId | None = None,
        param: str | None = None,
    ) -> Callable[[], None]:
        """Subscribe to changes, optionally filtered. Returns an unsubscriber."""
        sub = _Subscription(callback, channel, param)
        self._changes.append(sub)

        def unsubscribe() -> None:
            if sub in self._changes:
                self._changes.remove(sub)

        return unsubscribe

    def on_connection_state(self, cb: Callable[[ConnectionState], None]) -> None:
        self._connection.append(cb)

    def on_sync_complete(self, cb: Callable[[], None]) -> None:
        self._sync.append(cb)

    def on_write_unconfirmed(self, cb: Callable[[ChannelId, str, int | None], None]) -> None:
        self._unconfirmed.append(cb)

    def on_scene_recalled(self, cb: Callable[[int], None]) -> None:
        self._scene.append(cb)

    def on_meter_layout_error(self, cb: Callable[[str], None]) -> None:
        self._meter_layout.append(cb)

    # -- emission ---------------------------------------------------------

    def emit_change(self, event: ChangeEvent) -> None:
        for sub in list(self._changes):
            if sub.matches(event):
                self._call(sub.callback, event)

    def emit_connection_state(self, state: ConnectionState) -> None:
        for cb in list(self._connection):
            self._call(cb, state)

    def emit_sync_complete(self) -> None:
        for cb in list(self._sync):
            self._call(cb)

    def emit_write_unconfirmed(self, ch: ChannelId, param: str, sub: int | None) -> None:
        for cb in list(self._unconfirmed):
            self._call(cb, ch, param, sub)

    def emit_scene_recalled(self, scene: int) -> None:
        for cb in list(self._scene):
            self._call(cb, scene)

    def emit_meter_layout_error(self, message: str) -> None:
        for cb in list(self._meter_layout):
            self._call(cb, message)

    # -- dispatch ---------------------------------------------------------

    def _call(self, cb: Callable[..., None], *args: Any) -> None:
        started = self._now()
        try:
            cb(*args)
        except Exception:
            log.exception("event callback %r raised; continuing", getattr(cb, "__name__", cb))
        elapsed = self._now() - started
        if elapsed > self.slow_callback_warn:
            log.warning(
                "slow event callback %r took %.0f ms; callbacks run on the read loop and "
                "must not await long operations",
                getattr(cb, "__name__", cb),
                elapsed * 1000,
            )
```

### Task 4.3: Cache ingest, echo suppression and `UNCONFIRMED`

**Files:**
- Modify: `qumidi/client_async.py` (ingest, `get()`, echo expectations, `refresh()`)
- Test: `tests/test_state_sync.py`
- Test: `tests/test_echo.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `StateCache`, `EventBus`, `NrpnDecoder`, `REGISTRY`, `sub_index_for`.
- Produces:
  - `mixer.cache: StateCache` (read-only attribute), `mixer.events: EventBus`.
  - `mixer.get(ch, param, sub=None) -> CachedValue` — a synchronous, cheap cache read.
  - `mixer.membership(ch, param) -> MembershipSet`.
  - `await mixer.refresh(ch=None, param=None) -> None` — implemented as a full `resync()`.
  - `await mixer.resync() -> None`.
  - `mixer.on_change/on_connection_state/on_sync_complete/on_write_unconfirmed` delegating to the bus.
  - `echo_window: float = 1.0` constructor argument.

**Echo handling, all four cases the simple rule gets wrong (PRD §10.3):**
1. **Whether the Qu echoes at all is unverified.** Build nothing that *requires* an echo. `FakeQu` has an echo toggle and the state tests run under both settings.
2. **Duplicate expectations** are matched and consumed **FIFO, one echo per expectation**; a surplus echo after the queue is empty is treated as external.
3. **An expectation that expires unmatched** means the optimistic cache value was never confirmed — the mixer may have ignored the write (wrong `VX`, invalid on that class, console in Group mode). Downgrade to `UNCONFIRMED` and emit `write_unconfirmed`.
4. **A genuine surface move landing on the commanded value during the echo window** is indistinguishable from an echo and will be suppressed. Inherent to the protocol; document it and keep the window short.

**And the sync rule (PRD §9.3.8):** while `state = SYNCING`, tag every ingested update `source=SYNC` and emit no `external_change`. An operator moving a fader *during* a resync will have that move overwritten or swallowed — unavoidable, and documented.

```python
import asyncio

import pytest

from qumidi.client_async import AsyncQuMixer
from qumidi.errors import ValueUnknownError
from qumidi.events import Source
from qumidi.protocol.channels import Input, Mix
from qumidi.state import Freshness

SCENE = {
    (0x25, 0x17, 0x07): 0x62,   # Input 6 fader, 0 dB
    (0x25, 0x20, 0x02): 0x50,   # Input 6 send to Mix 3
    (0x67, 0x17, 0x07): 0x7F,   # LR fader
}


async def test_the_state_burst_populates_the_cache(fake_qu_factory) -> None:  # type: ignore[no-untyped-def]
    fake = await fake_qu_factory(scene=SCENE)
    async with AsyncQuMixer("127.0.0.1", port=fake.port) as mixer:
        await mixer.wait_synced()
        assert mixer.get(Input(6), "fader").raw == 0x62
        assert mixer.get(Input(6), "fader").freshness is Freshness.KNOWN
        assert mixer.get(Input(6), "send_level", 0x02).raw == 0x50
        assert mixer.get(Mix.LR, "fader").raw == 0x7F


async def test_the_last_parameter_of_the_burst_is_not_lost(fake_qu_factory) -> None:  # type: ignore[no-untyped-def]
    # PRD 9.3.7: flush the NRPN decoder's held update when 14 arrives, or the
    # cache is silently one value short after every sync.
    fake = await fake_qu_factory(scene=SCENE, drop_last_vx=True)
    async with AsyncQuMixer("127.0.0.1", port=fake.port) as mixer:
        await mixer.wait_synced()
        assert mixer.get(Mix.LR, "fader").raw == 0x7F


async def test_sync_emits_one_sync_complete_and_no_external_changes(fake_qu_factory) -> None:  # type: ignore[no-untyped-def]
    # PRD 9.3.4: do not emit external-change events for the burst; emit a
    # single SyncComplete at the end.
    fake = await fake_qu_factory(scene=SCENE)
    external: list[object] = []
    syncs: list[bool] = []
    mixer = AsyncQuMixer("127.0.0.1", port=fake.port)
    mixer.on_change(lambda e: external.append(e) if e.source is Source.MIXER else None)
    mixer.on_sync_complete(lambda: syncs.append(True))
    async with mixer:
        await mixer.wait_synced()
    assert external == []
    assert syncs == [True]


async def test_sync_sourced_changes_are_still_visible_to_subscribers(fake_qu_factory) -> None:  # type: ignore[no-untyped-def]
    fake = await fake_qu_factory(scene=SCENE)
    sourced: list[Source] = []
    mixer = AsyncQuMixer("127.0.0.1", port=fake.port)
    mixer.on_change(lambda e: sourced.append(e.source))
    async with mixer:
        await mixer.wait_synced()
    assert sourced and set(sourced) == {Source.SYNC}


async def test_a_surface_move_after_sync_fires_an_external_change(fake_qu_factory) -> None:  # type: ignore[no-untyped-def]
    # PRD 16.3.
    fake = await fake_qu_factory(scene=SCENE)
    seen: list[object] = []
    mixer = AsyncQuMixer("127.0.0.1", port=fake.port)
    async with mixer:
        await mixer.wait_synced()
        mixer.on_change(lambda e: seen.append(e) if e.source is Source.MIXER else None)
        await fake.send_surface_move(0x25, 0x17, 0x40, 0x07)
        await asyncio.sleep(0.1)
    assert len(seen) == 1
    assert mixer.get(Input(6), "fader").raw == 0x40


async def test_an_unknown_parameter_id_does_not_break_the_read_loop(fake_qu_factory) -> None:  # type: ignore[no-untyped-def]
    # PRD 5.3: an unrecognised (CH, ID) must not raise. Undocumented
    # parameters exist.
    fake = await fake_qu_factory(scene=SCENE)
    async with AsyncQuMixer("127.0.0.1", port=fake.port) as mixer:
        await mixer.wait_synced()
        await fake.send_surface_move(0x25, 0x7E, 0x01, 0x07)
        await asyncio.sleep(0.05)
        await fake.send_surface_move(0x25, 0x17, 0x30, 0x07)
        await asyncio.sleep(0.05)
        assert mixer.get(Input(6), "fader").raw == 0x30


async def test_refresh_is_documented_as_a_full_resync(fake_qu_factory) -> None:  # type: ignore[no-untyped-def]
    # PRD 10.2: no targeted get exists in the protocol.
    fake = await fake_qu_factory(scene=SCENE)
    async with AsyncQuMixer("127.0.0.1", port=fake.port) as mixer:
        await mixer.wait_synced()
        before = fake.sync_count
        await mixer.refresh(Input(6), "fader")
        assert fake.sync_count == before + 1
    assert "resync" in (AsyncQuMixer.refresh.__doc__ or "").lower()


async def test_reading_an_unknown_value_returns_unknown_not_a_number(fake_qu_factory) -> None:  # type: ignore[no-untyped-def]
    fake = await fake_qu_factory(scene={})
    async with AsyncQuMixer("127.0.0.1", port=fake.port) as mixer:
        await mixer.wait_synced()
        assert mixer.get(Input(6), "fader").freshness is Freshness.UNKNOWN
        assert mixer.get(Input(6), "fader").raw is None


async def test_membership_of_a_partially_known_dca_set_raises(fake_qu_factory) -> None:  # type: ignore[no-untyped-def]
    fake = await fake_qu_factory(scene={(0x25, 0x40, 0x07): 0x40})
    async with AsyncQuMixer("127.0.0.1", port=fake.port) as mixer:
        await mixer.wait_synced()
        with pytest.raises(ValueUnknownError):
            mixer.membership(Input(6), "dca_assign").require()
```

`tests/test_echo.py`:

```python
import asyncio

import pytest

from qumidi.client_async import AsyncQuMixer
from qumidi.events import Source
from qumidi.protocol.channels import Input
from qumidi.state import Freshness


@pytest.mark.parametrize("echo", [True, False])
async def test_a_write_never_fires_external_change(fake_qu_factory, echo) -> None:  # type: ignore[no-untyped-def]
    # PRD 16.3 and 10.3: a fader set via the API does not fire external_change,
    # whether or not the console echoes. Both must be exercised, because
    # whether the Qu echoes at all is unverified.
    fake = await fake_qu_factory(echo=echo)
    external: list[object] = []
    async with AsyncQuMixer("127.0.0.1", port=fake.port) as mixer:
        await mixer.wait_synced()
        mixer.on_change(lambda e: external.append(e) if e.source is Source.MIXER else None)
        await mixer.set(Input(6), "fader", raw=0x62)
        await asyncio.sleep(0.2)
    assert external == []


async def test_an_echo_confirms_the_optimistic_value(fake_qu_factory) -> None:  # type: ignore[no-untyped-def]
    fake = await fake_qu_factory(echo=True)
    async with AsyncQuMixer("127.0.0.1", port=fake.port) as mixer:
        await mixer.wait_synced()
        await mixer.set(Input(6), "fader", raw=0x62)
        await asyncio.sleep(0.15)
        value = mixer.get(Input(6), "fader")
        assert value.raw == 0x62
        assert value.freshness is Freshness.KNOWN


async def test_an_unmatched_expectation_downgrades_to_unconfirmed(fake_qu_factory) -> None:  # type: ignore[no-untyped-def]
    # PRD 16.11: the library never reports a value as KNOWN on the strength of
    # its own optimistic write alone.
    fake = await fake_qu_factory(echo=False)
    unconfirmed: list[tuple[object, str, object]] = []
    async with AsyncQuMixer("127.0.0.1", port=fake.port, echo_window=0.1) as mixer:
        await mixer.wait_synced()
        mixer.on_write_unconfirmed(lambda ch, p, s: unconfirmed.append((ch, p, s)))
        await mixer.set(Input(6), "fader", raw=0x62)
        assert mixer.get(Input(6), "fader").freshness is Freshness.KNOWN  # optimistic
        await asyncio.sleep(0.3)
        assert mixer.get(Input(6), "fader").freshness is Freshness.UNCONFIRMED
        assert mixer.get(Input(6), "fader").raw == 0x62
    assert unconfirmed == [(Input(6), "fader", None)]


async def test_duplicate_expectations_are_consumed_fifo(fake_qu_factory) -> None:  # type: ignore[no-untyped-def]
    # PRD 10.3: two writes of the same value produce two expectations and
    # possibly one echo. Match one echo per expectation.
    fake = await fake_qu_factory(echo=False)
    external: list[object] = []
    async with AsyncQuMixer("127.0.0.1", port=fake.port, echo_window=1.0) as mixer:
        await mixer.wait_synced()
        mixer.on_change(lambda e: external.append(e) if e.source is Source.MIXER else None)
        await mixer.set(Input(6), "fader", raw=0x62)
        await mixer.set(Input(6), "fader", raw=0x62)
        await fake.send_surface_move(0x25, 0x17, 0x62, 0x07)
        await asyncio.sleep(0.1)
        assert external == []  # consumed the first expectation
        await fake.send_surface_move(0x25, 0x17, 0x62, 0x07)
        await asyncio.sleep(0.1)
        assert external == []  # consumed the second
        await fake.send_surface_move(0x25, 0x17, 0x62, 0x07)
        await asyncio.sleep(0.1)
        assert len(external) == 1  # surplus echo is external


async def test_a_different_value_arriving_is_external_not_an_echo(fake_qu_factory) -> None:  # type: ignore[no-untyped-def]
    fake = await fake_qu_factory(echo=False)
    external: list[object] = []
    async with AsyncQuMixer("127.0.0.1", port=fake.port, echo_window=1.0) as mixer:
        await mixer.wait_synced()
        mixer.on_change(lambda e: external.append(e) if e.source is Source.MIXER else None)
        await mixer.set(Input(6), "fader", raw=0x62)
        await fake.send_surface_move(0x25, 0x17, 0x40, 0x07)
        await asyncio.sleep(0.1)
    assert len(external) == 1
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Add the `FakeQu` helpers the tests need**

```python
    async def send_surface_move(self, ch: int, param_id: int, value: int, vx: int) -> None:
        """Emit an unsolicited NRPN, as a physical control move would."""
        if self._client is None:
            raise RuntimeError("no client connected")
        self.state[(ch, param_id, vx)] = value
        self._client.write(encode_nrpn(self.midi_channel, ch, param_id, value, vx))
        await self._client.drain()
```

Add `self.sync_count = 0` in `__init__`, incremented at the top of `_do_sync`. Add a `drop_last_vx: bool = False` keyword; in `_push_state`, when set, emit the final entry's first three CCs only, so the held-update flush is exercised:

```python
    async def _push_state(self, writer: asyncio.StreamWriter) -> None:
        items = list(self._scene.items())
        for index, ((ch, param_id, vx), value) in enumerate(items):
            data = encode_nrpn(self.midi_channel, ch, param_id, value, vx)
            if self.drop_last_vx and index == len(items) - 1:
                # PRD 9.3.7: a burst ending without its Data LSB. The client
                # must flush the held update when 14 arrives.
                data = data[:9]
            writer.write(data)
        await writer.drain()
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement ingest, reads and echo handling in `AsyncQuMixer`**

Add to `__init__`:

```python
        self.echo_window = echo_window
        self.cache = StateCache()
        self.events = EventBus()
        self._decoder = NrpnDecoder()
        self._expectations: dict[CacheKey, deque[tuple[int, float]]] = defaultdict(deque)
        self._expectation_tasks: set[asyncio.Task[None]] = set()
        self._syncing = False
        self._transport.on_state_change(self.events.emit_connection_state)
```

with a new keyword `echo_window: float = c.ECHO_WINDOW`.

Reads and subscriptions:

```python
    def get(self, ch: ChannelId, param: str, sub: int | None = None) -> CachedValue:
        """Read the cache. Synchronous and cheap in both APIs (PRD 10.2)."""
        return self.cache.get(ch, param, sub)

    def membership(self, ch: ChannelId, param: str) -> MembershipSet:
        """Read a set-valued parameter (DCA / mute-group assign, PRD 10.1a)."""
        spec = REGISTRY.lookup(param, ch.cls)
        if not isinstance(spec.domain, FlaggedIndex):
            raise UnknownParameterError(f"{param} is not a set-valued parameter")
        return self.cache.membership(ch, param, spec.domain.count, spec.domain.on_flag)

    def on_change(self, cb, channel=None, param=None):  # type: ignore[no-untyped-def]
        return self.events.on_change(cb, channel=channel, param=param)

    def on_connection_state(self, cb) -> None:  # type: ignore[no-untyped-def]
        self.events.on_connection_state(cb)

    def on_sync_complete(self, cb) -> None:  # type: ignore[no-untyped-def]
        self.events.on_sync_complete(cb)

    def on_write_unconfirmed(self, cb) -> None:  # type: ignore[no-untyped-def]
        self.events.on_write_unconfirmed(cb)

    async def refresh(self, ch: ChannelId | None = None, param: str | None = None) -> None:
        """Re-read state from the console.

        The protocol offers no targeted get, so this is implemented as a full
        resync regardless of the arguments (PRD 10.2). The arguments exist
        only to document intent at the call site.
        """
        await self.resync()

    async def resync(self) -> None:
        """Re-run the full state handshake against a live connection."""
        await self._sync()
```

Ingest, driven from `_dispatch`:

```python
    def _dispatch(self, msg: MidiMessage) -> None:
        if isinstance(msg, SysEx):
            for update in self._decoder.feed(msg):
                self._ingest(update)
            self._on_sysex(msg)
            return
        for update in self._decoder.feed(msg):
            self._ingest(update)

    def _ingest(self, update: RawParamUpdate) -> None:
        if self._midi_channel is not None and update.midi_channel != self._midi_channel:
            # MIDI strip traffic lives on N+1 and is a different namespace
            # entirely (PRD 6.3, 14.7). Slice 11 surfaces it.
            return
        try:
            ch = channel_from_raw(update.ch)
        except ParameterRangeError:
            log.debug("update for unassigned CH %#04x; ignoring", update.ch)
            return
        spec = REGISTRY.resolve(ch.cls, update.param_id, update.vx)
        if spec is None:
            # PRD 5.3: an unrecognised (CH, ID) must not raise. Undocumented
            # parameters exist.
            log.debug(
                "unrecognised parameter: CH %#04x ID %#04x VX %s",
                update.ch, update.param_id, update.vx,
            )
            return
        raw = spec.domain.clamp_inbound(update.value, spec.name, ch.label)
        sub = sub_index_for(spec, update.vx, raw)

        if self._syncing:
            old, new = self.cache.set(ch, spec.name, sub, raw, Freshness.KNOWN)
            self.events.emit_change(
                ChangeEvent(ch, spec.name, sub, old, new, Source.SYNC)
            )
            return

        if self._consume_expectation(ch, spec.name, sub, raw):
            # An echo of our own write: update the cache, emit no external
            # change, so a fader-follow script cannot oscillate (PRD 10.3).
            old, new = self.cache.set(ch, spec.name, sub, raw, Freshness.KNOWN)
            self.events.emit_change(
                ChangeEvent(ch, spec.name, sub, old, new, Source.LOCAL)
            )
            return

        old, new = self.cache.set(ch, spec.name, sub, raw, Freshness.KNOWN)
        self.events.emit_change(ChangeEvent(ch, spec.name, sub, old, new, Source.MIXER))

    def _consume_expectation(
        self, ch: ChannelId, param: str, sub: int | None, raw: int
    ) -> bool:
        """FIFO, one echo per expectation (PRD 10.3)."""
        key = self.cache.key(ch, param, sub)
        queue = self._expectations.get(key)
        if not queue:
            return False
        if queue[0][0] != raw:
            return False
        queue.popleft()
        return True
```

The optimistic write and its expiry, called from `set()` after `_send` succeeds:

```python
    def _expect_echo(self, ch: ChannelId, param: str, sub: int | None, raw: int) -> None:
        key = self.cache.key(ch, param, sub)
        deadline = time.monotonic() + self.echo_window
        self._expectations[key].append((raw, deadline))
        task = asyncio.create_task(self._expire_expectation(ch, param, sub, raw))
        self._expectation_tasks.add(task)
        task.add_done_callback(self._expectation_tasks.discard)

    async def _expire_expectation(
        self, ch: ChannelId, param: str, sub: int | None, raw: int
    ) -> None:
        await asyncio.sleep(self.echo_window)
        key = self.cache.key(ch, param, sub)
        queue = self._expectations.get(key)
        if not queue:
            return
        for index, (value, _) in enumerate(queue):
            if value == raw:
                del queue[index]
                break
        else:
            return
        # PRD 10.3: the mixer may have ignored the write -- wrong VX, invalid
        # on this channel class, console in Group mode. Do not leave the value
        # looking KNOWN.
        self.cache.downgrade(ch, param, sub, Freshness.UNCONFIRMED)
        log.info(
            "write to %s %s was never confirmed by the console; the message may have "
            "been ignored (check VX, channel class, and Group mode -- PRD 14.6)",
            ch.label, param,
        )
        self.events.emit_write_unconfirmed(ch, param, sub)

    def _clear_expectations(self) -> None:
        """PRD 4.4: drop pending write expectations on disconnect, or the
        first post-reconnect surface move is misclassified as an echo."""
        for task in self._expectation_tasks:
            task.cancel()
        self._expectation_tasks.clear()
        self._expectations.clear()
```

At the end of `set()`:

```python
        spec_resolved = REGISTRY.lookup(param, ch.cls)
        vx_byte = self._resolve_vx(spec_resolved, vx)
        sub = sub_index_for(spec_resolved, vx_byte, raw)
        old, new = self.cache.set(ch, spec_resolved.name, sub, raw, Freshness.KNOWN)
        self.events.emit_change(
            ChangeEvent(ch, spec_resolved.name, sub, old, new, Source.LOCAL)
        )
        self._expect_echo(ch, spec_resolved.name, sub, raw)
```

In `_sync()`, wrap the burst window and flush the decoder:

```python
        self._syncing = True
        try:
            ...  # existing send + wait logic
        finally:
            self._syncing = False
```

and in `_on_sysex`, on `CMD_END_OF_SYNC`, flush before setting the event:

```python
        elif parsed.command == c.CMD_END_OF_SYNC:
            # PRD 9.3.7: flush the held update, or the last parameter in the
            # burst is stranded and the cache is silently one value short.
            for update in self._decoder.flush():
                self._ingest(update)
            self._sync_event.set()
            self.events.emit_sync_complete()
```

Add a periodic decoder tick so trigger (c) — 50 ms of inbound silence — actually fires. Start it in `connect()` and cancel it in `close()`:

```python
    async def _tick_loop(self) -> None:
        while True:
            await asyncio.sleep(c.PENDING_FLUSH_TIMEOUT)
            for update in self._decoder.tick():
                self._ingest(update)
```

In `close()`, also call `self._clear_expectations()` and `self._decoder.reset()`.

Imports: `time`, `from collections import defaultdict, deque`, `from qumidi.state import CachedValue, Freshness, StateCache, MembershipSet, CacheKey, sub_index_for`, `from qumidi.events import ChangeEvent, EventBus, Source`, `from qumidi.midi.nrpn import NrpnDecoder, RawParamUpdate`, `from qumidi.protocol.channels import channel_from_raw`, `from qumidi.protocol.params import FlaggedIndex`.

```python
async def cmd_monitor(args: argparse.Namespace) -> int:
    from qumidi.events import ChangeEvent, Source
    from qumidi.protocol.channels import parse_channel_ref

    channel = parse_channel_ref(args.channel) if args.channel else None

    def show(event: ChangeEvent) -> None:
        if event.source is Source.SYNC and not args.include_sync:
            return
        tag = {Source.LOCAL: "local", Source.MIXER: "SURFACE", Source.SYNC: "sync"}[event.source]
        sub = "" if event.sub_index is None else f"[{event.sub_index}]"
        old = "?" if event.old.raw is None else f"{event.old.raw:#04x}"
        print(
            f"{tag:8} {event.channel.label:<14} {event.param}{sub:<6} "
            f"{old} -> {event.new.raw:#04x}"
        )

    async with AsyncQuMixer(args.host, port=args.port) as mixer:
        await mixer.wait_synced()
        mixer.on_change(show, channel=channel, param=args.param)
        print(f"monitoring {args.host}; move something on the console. Ctrl-C to stop.")
        await asyncio.Event().wait()
    return 0


async def cmd_dump_state(args: argparse.Namespace) -> int:
    import json

    async with AsyncQuMixer(args.host, port=args.port) as mixer:
        await mixer.wait_synced()
        entries = []
        for (raw_ch, cls, param, sub), value in sorted(
            mixer.cache.items(), key=lambda item: (item[0][0], item[0][2], item[0][3] or -1)
        ):
            entries.append(
                {
                    "channel_raw": raw_ch,
                    "channel_class": cls.name,
                    "param": param,
                    "sub_index": sub,
                    "raw": value.raw,
                    "freshness": value.freshness.name,
                }
            )
        if args.json:
            print(json.dumps(
                {"box_id": mixer.box_id, "firmware": mixer.firmware, "values": entries},
                indent=2,
            ))
        else:
            from qumidi.protocol.channels import channel_from_raw

            print(f"BoxID {mixer.box_id}, firmware {mixer.firmware}, {len(entries)} values")
            for entry in entries:
                label = channel_from_raw(int(entry["channel_raw"])).label  # type: ignore[arg-type]
                sub = "" if entry["sub_index"] is None else f"[{entry['sub_index']}]"
                print(
                    f"  {label:<14} {entry['param']}{sub:<6} "
                    f"{entry['raw']:#04x}  {entry['freshness']}"
                )
    return 0
```

and in `build_parser()`:

```python
    mon = sub.add_parser("monitor", help="print state changes as they arrive")
    mon.add_argument("--host", required=True)
    mon.add_argument("--port", type=int, default=c.QU_TCP_PORT)
    mon.add_argument("--channel", help="filter, e.g. input:6")
    mon.add_argument("--param", help="filter, e.g. fader")
    mon.add_argument("--include-sync", action="store_true", help="also show the sync burst")
    mon.set_defaults(func=cmd_monitor)

    dump = sub.add_parser("dump-state", help="sync, print the whole cache, exit")
    dump.add_argument("--host", required=True)
    dump.add_argument("--port", type=int, default=c.QU_TCP_PORT)
    dump.add_argument("--json", action="store_true")
    dump.set_defaults(func=cmd_dump_state)
```
